# The line bank: what the people in a story say about it, more versions
# (dialogue D2; format in src/world/folk/voice.rs). Same keys as
# storylines.txt: story.quote.<family>.<part>.<up|down>, then
# story.quote.<family>.<part>, then story.quote.any.<part>. The speaker is a
# player (tag player) or a coach (tag coach); a version with neither must fit
# both. Up is good news for his team, down is bad. Facts: {team} {rival}
# {rival_last} {lead} {lead_last} {other} {coach} {n} {record} {since}. In a
# lead's quote the rival is {rival_last}; in a rival's quote the lead is
# {lead_last}. Said without quote marks; the reader adds "Smith said."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- anyone
